Maybe implement a simple but robust loop:
- Normalize a.
- For iter in range(max_iter until time):
   b=M from current (cache)
   choose w from b (beta schedule, k list)
   H=corr(a,w); d=H/M-1
   for eta in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]: q=normalize(a*exp(-eta*clip(d,-10,10))); score=exact(q); if best update, choose best q as current if score<current.
   update current to best q from candidates (or if none, keep)
   occasionally resample parent/modes.

Let's revisit the LP state-of-art. It might be intended for this problem and can find optimum. The previous code used LSE but not LP. Could implement LP step with scipy for moderate size and multiple iterations. Maybe that will converge quickly to 1.5027. The previous state of art described: solve LP maximizing sum g subject to f*g ≤ M, update. If we implement exact LP for n maybe 2048 and iterate, we can improve. Let's understand LP update mathematically.
Given f normalized? They normalize sum sqrt(2n). M=||f*f||∞. Define g≥0 maximizing sum g subject to ||f*g||∞ ≤ M. Then rescale g to same sum, update f=(1-t)f+t g. This is like a projected step: g is the farthest point (in sum) in sublevel set of convolution with f. Since f*g constraints are linear in g. The objective f has max conv M. For any such g, consider h=α f + β g? They mix. It should reduce max conv.
We can implement this with linprog for n maybe 1024-2048. But final length maybe 10240. Could do LP for smaller n to improve a low-res pattern, then interpolate to final and polish. Or run LP directly for n=10240 using sparse matrix and scipy linprog? Let's estimate: Constraints 20479, vars 10240. A matrix banded: each column g_j appears in constraints indices j..j+n-1 with coefficients f_{k-j}. Dense 20479*10240 ~209M floats 1.7GB too high. But scipy linprog can take scipy sparse? The HiGHS C in scipy may accept LinearConstraint or sparse A_ub? In scipy.optimize.linprog, A_ub can be sparse (scipy.sparse) since recent. But solver may convert? It supports sparse for method 'highs'. Could be memory okay (~209M nonzeros? Wait each column length n, total nonzeros (2n-1)*? Actually A_{k,j}=f_{k-j} if 0≤k-j<n. For each row k, number nonzero min(k+1,n,2n-1-k). Total nonzeros = n(2n-1)? Let's compute: row length up to n, total ~n², 100M nonzeros. Each float 8 + index ~12 => >1GB. Too high for 1 CPU maybe. For n=2048 total nonzeros ~4M, dense 32MB, okay. LP with 4095 constraints, 2048 vars, 4M nonzeros maybe okay. We can run many? Maybe 1-5s per LP with HiGHS? Could be too slow but maybe 2400s allows hundreds. We can use n=2048 for LP, then resample to 16384. But resampling smooth may lose structure.

Alternatively, define LP variable g length n but constraints only a subset (active/important) to reduce size? The original uses all 2n-1 but many not binding. Could use active set from current f: constraints at top lags plus maybe all with margins? For LP to push sum, only constraints that will bind matter; we can solve with current all? We can approximate by using a random subset or central constraints. But maybe need all to avoid peaks.

We can implement a custom projected gradient for LP: maximize sum g with constraints A g ≤ M. The gradient of objective is 1. Use dual y≥0; KKT: 1 - A^T y + μ=0. Since A^T y is convolution with y. y support length n. This is a yy? We can solve for y via projected gradient? The dual is convex: minimize M∑y s.t. B y ≥1, y≥0, where B_{j,k}=f_{j-k}. This is linear. We can use scipy.optimize.linprog on transpose smaller? Variables y length n, constraints 2n-1, matrix B same nonzeros. For n=2048 okay. But maybe not needed.

Maybe use the LP step in a small-resolution evolution: start from uniform length 1024, run LP iterations to improve score at n. Then upsample to 4096/16384 and polish with mirror. Since the optimal shape likely smooth, LP at low n can find pattern.

Utilities:

- fast_self_conv(a): FFT length next_pow2(2n-1)
- corr_pair(a,w): returns H_i = ∑ a_j w_{i+j}, i=0..n-1, w length 2n-1. Use FFT: H_i =∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. This is cross-correlation of a and w? Let's derive efficient: Let A = a reversed? Code previous: A = rfft(a[::-1], N), W = rfft(w, N), full=irfft(A*W,N)[:M], return full[n-1:2n-1]. Let's verify. For i=0, want ∑_j a_j w_j. a[::-1]_{n-1-j}=a_j. Cross-correlation full[t] =∑_{u} a[::-1]_u w_{u+t?} maybe. The previous used full[n-1:2n-1]. We'll trust; or implement direct using np.correlate? n large but FFT.
Alternative derive: We need H_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}. Define array A_rev of length N with A_rev[n-1-j]=a_j. Define W length N with W[k]=w_k. Linear convolution C = A_rev * W: C[m]=∑_{u} A_rev[u] W[m-u]. Set m=n-1+i. Then A_rev[u]=a_{n-1-u}. Let j=n-1-u => u=n-1-j. W[m-u]=W[n-1+i - (n-1-j)]=W[i+j]. Sum =∑_j a_j w[i+j]. Yes H_i=C[n-1+i]. Good. So conv = irfft(rfft(A_rev,N)*rfft(W,N)); return C[n-1:n+n-1].
- normalize(a,target=1): clip NaN, nonneg, sum.
- resample_sequence(a,n): linear interpolation.
- exact_score(a): use fast_self_conv, sum.

Multiplicative descent (MD):
Function md_polish(a, deadline, max_iters, beta_list/k_list, step_list):
  a=normalize(a); current=score(a); best.
  Use log_a = log(a+eps) to update.
  Cache b,M? For each iter:
    b=fast_self_conv(a); M=max(b)
    if M<=0 break
    # weights
    for variant: top-k and softmax
      w = weights_from_b(b, M, mode)
      H=corr_pair(a,w)
      d = H / M - 1.0
      # Optional: average out constant: d = d - np.mean(d) maybe to avoid scale; but M target. Use clip.
      for eta in steps:
         logq = log_a - eta * clip(d, -maxd, maxd)
         q = normalize(exp(logq - maxlogq))
         sc=exact_score(q)
         if sc < best: update best; if sc < current: current=sc; a=q; log_a=log(q+eps); break? Choose candidate with best among eta then continue.
    To avoid too many exact scores, choose a few eta. For n=16384, exact score ~ one FFT. We can do e.g., modes 4, topks 4, etas 3 => 12 scores/iter. 1000 iter =12000 FFT maybe? FFT 16384 ~0.002s? 12000*0.002=24s plus conv maybe 0.01? Actually exact score uses FFT O(NlogN) ~32768*15 ~0.5M ops, ~0.01s; 12000=120s. OK.
  But line search exact each candidate. We can reduce.

Block custom optimization:

```
def multiplicative_polish(a, deadline, beta_values=None, k_fracs=None, eta_values=None, max_iters=...):
    a=normalize(a)
    cur=exact_score(a); best=a.copy(); bestsc=cur
    loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
    m=len(a); L=2*m-1
    k_list = unique fractions * L: [0, 1,3,5,10,20,50,100,200,500,1000,2000,5000, L//10, L//4]
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time...
       b=fast_self_conv(a); M=b.max()
       if not finite or M<=0: break
       # weights list
       progress=it/max_iters
       for k in adaptive k_list:
          w=topk(b,k)
          H=corr_pair(a,w)
          d=H/M -1
          # Optional center: d -= mean(d) ? If use M target, no. But if support all and H average? Maybe subtract mean to keep sum? Let's not.
          d=np.clip(d,-10,10)
          # try eta
          for eta in eta_values:
             # if d has large negative, exp may overflow; use loga
             logq=loga - eta*d
             logq = logq - max(logq)
             if min(logq) < -700: maybe clip
             q=normalize(exp(logq))
             sc=exact_score(q)
             if sc < bestsc: best...
             if sc < cur: cur=sc; a=q; loga=np.log(q+1e-300); break
       # if no improvement for several iters, break or perturb
```
This uses exact score many times. We can precompute H for softmax weights too. We can include top-k with k small: for k=1, H is one lag. For k large, ok.

Correct LSE implementation:
```
def lse_score_grad(a, beta, pen=1e-3):
  n=len(a); S=a.sum()
  if S<1e-8 return inf zeros
  c=fast_self_conv(a)
  d=n*c/(S*S)
  z=beta*d
  maxz=max(z); ez=exp(z-maxz); sumez=...
  L=(maxz+log(sumez))/beta
  w=ez/sumez
  H=corr_pair(a,w)
  LDS=float(dot(w,d))
  score=2*L + pen*(S-1)**2
  grad=4*n*H/(S*S) - 4*LDS*a/S + 2*pen*(S-1)*ones
  return score, grad
```
Check derivative: d = n c/S². ∂logsumexp(βd)/∂a = ∑ softmax_j ∂d_j. ∂d_j/S²: n(2a_{j-i}/S²) -2n c_j a_i/S³ = 2n a_{j-i}/S² -2 d_j a_i/S. Multiply softmax sum: 2nH/S² -2a_i LDS/S. Score 2L => 4nH/S² -4a_i LDS/S. Good.
Penalty for S? If we want S=1, yes. But objective scale invariant; penalty small. Since final we normalize, ok.
`run_lbfgs`: use minimize with jit? no. Provide bounds [0,10]. Need maxfun dynamic. Use `options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol':1e-15,...}`.
But if n=16384, bounds list of 16384 tuples okay. L-BFGS memory default maybe uses m=10; each vector 16k, fine. However scipy L-BFGS with finite? We provide jac. It may call many evals. Use estimate.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb(a, rng, sigma):
  n=len(a)
  mode = rng.integers(0,6)
  if mode0: q=a*exp(sigma*rng.normal(n))
  if mode1: q=(1-t)a + t*resample(parent?) or uniform
  if mode2: reverse
  if mode3: log smoothed noise
  if mode4: add sinusoidal
  return normalize(clip(q,0,None))
```
Need no closures.

```
def generate_pattern(n, kind, params, rng):
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  if uniform: ones
  linear: 2*x or 2*(1-x) or 1+α(2x-1)
  vshape: 1 - α|x-0.5|? anti? Use 1+α(2x-1)
  sine: 1+α sin(2πm x), 1+α cos...
  block: levels from shape, repeat
  random smooth: FFT noise
  piecewise trapezoid: p=1+α*clip((0.5-x)/width,-1,1) anti with flat ends. This can reduce endpoint peaks. Try width 0.1,0.2,0.25,0.33.
  two-level step optimized: high block length 1/3, low rest, high/low ratio r from [1.5,2,2.4,3].
  three-level step: high length α, mid, low; generate random α.
```

Let's optimize continuous trapezoid alpha, width. We can include in code a quick 2D grid search evaluating discrete sequences. This is simple and likely produce strong candidate.

Define `make_trapezoid(n, w_frac, alpha)`:
 x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n perhaps.
 f = (0.5 - x)/w_frac
 f = clip(f, -1, 1)  # high left, low right
 p = 1 + alpha*f
 p = clip(p,0,None)
 # Since mean of clipped f not exactly 0 if plateaus? For clip of odd anti function, mean is 0 because f(1-x)=-f(x) (exact with symmetric x). With x grid symmetric if n even? use x=(i+0.5)/n, f anti? f(1-x) = -f(x) if x grid symmetric. For simplicity, normalize p sum to1. It will be near.
 return normalize(p)
```
But if we normalize p, the mean shifts, anti property slight broken, okay.
```

```
Pattern generation:
```
def make_trap(n, w, alpha):
  x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  f=(0.5-x)/max(w,1e-6)
  f=np.clip(f,-1,1)
  p=1.0+alpha*f
  p=np.clip(p,0.0,None)
  if p.sum()<1e-12: p=np.ones(n)
  return normalize(p)
```
Reverse: make_trap with alpha negative? `f_reversed = (x-0.5)/w` equivalent alpha negative. We'll include alpha signs.

```

Pseudo:
```
def multiplicative_polish(a_init, deadline, max_candidates_per_iter=3, verbose=False):
  a=normalize(a_init); cur=exact_score(a); best=a.copy(); bestsc=cur; loga=np.log(a+1e-300)
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  topks=...
  specs=[]
  noise=0
  stall=0
  it=0
  while time.monotonic()<deadline:
    b=fast_self_conv(a); M=max(b)
    if M<=0: break
    improved=False
    # specs selection
    for j in range(max_candidates_per_iter):
       spec=specs[(it+j)%len]
       w=get_weight(spec,b,M,L)
       H=corr_pair(a,w)
       d=H/M
       d-=np.median(d)
       d=np.clip(d,-8,8)
       # choose etas
       for eta in (0.05,0.15,0.3):
          logq=loga - eta*d
          # clip logq range? normalize handles
          q=normalize(np.exp(logq - np.max(logq)))
          sc=exact_score(q)
          if sc<bestsc: best=q.copy(); bestsc=sc; improved=True
          if sc<cur: cur=sc; a=q; loga=np.log(np.maximum(q,1e-300)); break
    it+=1; if not improved: stall+=1 else stall=0
    if stall>30:
       # perturb
       a=perturb(a,rng,0.1); loga=...
       cur=exact_score(a); if cur<bestsc update
       stall=0
       it=0
  return bestsc,best
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

This implementation combines:
- a coarse parametric/grid search over asymmetric trapezoids, sinusoids,
  and random smooth shapes;
- smooth high-beta LSE polishing with a corrected gradient;
- exact multiplicative mirror-descent polishing using exact max
  subgradients and adaptive active sets;
- bounded random restarts and final cleanup.

The goal is to beat the previous SOTA (1.506382) and approach the
target upper bound 1.5027.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

PEN_SUM = 1.0e-2


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Return H_i = sum_j a_j w_{i+j}, for i=0..len(a)-1,
    where w has length 2*len(a)-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    a_rev = np.empty(N, dtype=np.float64)
    a_rev[:n] = a[::-1]
    W = np.empty(N, dtype=np.float64)
    W[:m] = w
    out = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a_rev, N) * np.fft.rfft(W, N), N)[:M]
    return out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if a.size == 0 or S < 1e-12:
        n = max(1, int(a.size))
        return np.full(n, float(target) / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (float(target) / S)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                      nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-8:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 100.0

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (S * S)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)

    z = beta * d
    zmax = float(np.max(z)) if d.size else 0.0
    ez = np.exp(np.clip(z - zmax, -700.0, 700.0))
    sz = float(ez.sum())
    if sz <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sz):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = ez / sz
    L = (zmax + float(np.log(sz))) / beta
    H = corr_pair(a, w)
    LDS = float(np.dot(w, d))

    score = 2.0 * L + PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0) ** 2
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * H) / (S * S)
    grad -= (4.0 * LDS) * a / S
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e6, 1e6)
    return float(score), grad.astype(np.float64)


def fun_lse(a, beta):
    return lse_score_grad(a, float(beta))


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline, maxfun=200):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    sc0 = exact_score(a)

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 5.0:
        return sc0, a

    if minimize is None:
        return sc0, a

    rem = max(5.0, rem)
    est_eval = max(1e-3, 0.025 * (float(n) / 10240.0))
    mf = int(min(350, max(64, (0.8 * rem) / est_eval)))
    mf = min(int(maxfun), mf)
    maxiter = max(50, mf)

    bounds = [(0.0, 10.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-15,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(mf),
        "maxls": 50,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun_lse,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return sc0, a

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        a_out = normalize_a(res.x)
    else:
        a_out = a

    sc = exact_score(a_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        a_out = a
        sc = sc0

    return float(sc), a_out


def topk_weights(b, k):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(b.size)
    k = int(k)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if k <= 0 or k >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    if k == 1:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return w
    idx = np.argpartition(b, L - k)[L - k:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def margin_weights(b, m, margin):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(b.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    thr = float(m) * (1.0 - float(margin))
    mask = b >= thr
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    if cnt <= 0:
        return topk_weights(b, 1)
    if cnt >= L:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    w[mask] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
    return w


def softmax_weights(b, m, beta):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = int(b.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-6:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    z = np.clip(beta * (b - m), -700.0, 700.0)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return topk_weights(b, 1)
    return e / s


def make_weight_from_spec(spec, b, m, L):
    kind = int(spec[0])
    val = spec[1]
    if kind == 0:
        return topk_weights(b, int(val))
    if kind == 1:
        return margin_weights(b, m, float(val))
    return softmax_weights(b, m, float(val))


def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=0.2):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    strength = float(max(1e-4, min(1.0, strength)))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        q = a * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = a[::-1].copy()
        q = q * np.exp(0.15 * strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        # cheap smoothing by repeated average if n is large
        if n > 1024:
            k = min(31, max(3, int(n) // 512))
            if k % 2 == 0:
                k += 1
            kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
            noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        q = np.exp(loga + strength * 0.25 * noise)
    elif mode == 4:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        f = int(rng.integers(1, 13))
        q = a * (1.0 + strength * 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
    else:
        q = a + strength * 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)

    return normalize_a(q)


def make_trap(n, w_frac, alpha):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    w = float(max(1e-4, w_frac))
    f = (0.5 - x) / w
    f = np.clip(f, -1.0, 1.0)
    p = 1.0 + float(alpha) * f
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / S
    return p


def make_sine(n, freq, alpha, phase=0.0):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    p = 1.0 + float(alpha) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * int(freq) * x + float(phase))
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / S
    return p


def make_random_smooth(n, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    N = next_power_of_two(n)
    freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(N) * N
    power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.2, 3.5)))
    power[0] = 0.0
    phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
    spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
    noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, N)[:n]
    noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
    amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9))
    p = 1.0 + amp * noise
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / S
    return p


def update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    a = normalize_a(a)
    k = len(top_scores)
    pos = int(np.argmax(top_scores))
    if sc < top_scores[pos]:
        top_scores[pos] = float(sc)
        top_as[pos] = a.copy()
        i = pos
        while i > 0:
            if top_scores[i] < top_scores[i - 1]:
                top_scores[i], top_scores[i - 1] = top_scores[i - 1], top_scores[i]
                top_as[i], top_as[i - 1] = top_as[i - 1], top_as[i]
                i -= 1
            else:
                break


def multiplicative_polish(a_init, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_a(a_init)
    cur = exact_score(a)
    best = a.copy()
    bestsc = cur

    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))

    specs = []
    for k in (1, 2, 3, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000, 2000, 5000, 10000):
        if k < L:
            specs.append((0, k))
    for m in (0.01, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50):
        specs.append((1, m))
    for beta in (0.25, 1.0, 5.0, 20.0):
        specs.append((2, beta))
    if not specs:
        specs = [(1, 0.5)]

    etas = (0.08, 0.03, 0.20)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if it % 160 == 0 and time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0:
            print("  MDE iter {} best={:.9f} cur={:.9f}".format(it, bestsc, cur))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        b = fast_self_conv(a)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        improved = False
        per_iter = 3

        for jj in range(per_iter):
            spec = specs[(it + jj) % len(specs)]
            w = make_weight_from_spec(spec, b, M, L)
            H = corr_pair(a, w)
            if H.size != n:
                continue

            d = H / M
            d = d - float(np.median(d))
            d = np.clip(d, -8.0, 8.0)
            d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=8.0, neginf=-8.0)

            for eta in etas:
                logq = loga - float(eta) * d
                logq = np.nan_to_num(logq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-1e6)
                logq = logq - float(np.max(logq))
                q = np.exp(np.clip(logq, -800.0, 800.0))
                q = normalize_a(q)
                sc = exact_score(q)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if sc < bestsc:
                    bestsc = float(sc)
                    best = q.copy()
                    improved = True
                if sc < cur:
                    cur = float(sc)
                    a = q
                    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                    break

        it += 1
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 45:
                a = perturb_best(best, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.15, 0.45)))
                cur = exact_score(a)
                if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < bestsc:
                    bestsc = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                if not np.isfinite(cur) or cur > bestsc + 1e-6:
                    a = best.copy()
                    cur = bestsc
                loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
                stall = 0

    return float(bestsc), best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(min(2400.0, max(60.0, float(budget_s))))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 20.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(np.sum(p)) > 1e-9:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and 4096 <= parent.size <= 12288:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 10240

    print("Length: {}, parent: {}".format(n, parent is not None))

    top_scores = [np.inf, np.inf, np.inf, np.inf, np.inf]
    top_as = [None, None, None, None, None]

    def add_candidate(a):
        # local helper-free by using inline outside? This nested function is a closure.
        # To obey no closures, we do not define nested functions.
        pass

    # Uniform.
    a = normalize_a(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    sc = exact_score(a)
    update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)

    # Parent.
    if parent is not None:
        a = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n))
        sc = exact_score(a)
        update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)
        a = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent[::-1], n))
        sc = exact_score(a)
        update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)

    # Asymmetric trapezoid grid. This is a strong global basin finder.
    ws = np.arange(0.05, 0.5 + 1e-9, 0.025)
    alphas = np.arange(0.10, 1.0 + 1e-9, 0.10)
    considered = 2
    for wv in ws:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1500:
            break
        for av in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1500:
                break
            av = float(abs(av))
            for sign in (1.0, -1.0):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1500:
                    break
                a = normalize_a(make_trap(n, float(wv), sign * av))
                sc = exact_score(a)
                update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)
                considered += 1
                if considered % 80 == 0:
                    print("  initial candidates considered={}, best={:.9f}".format(considered, top_scores[0]))

    # Sinusoidal anti/other modes.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1400:
        for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12):
            for av in (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0):
                for phase in (0.0, 0.25 * np.pi):
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1400:
                        break
                    a = normalize_a(make_sine(n, f, float(av), float(phase)))
                    sc = exact_score(a)
                    update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)

    # Random smooth shapes.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1300:
        for _ in range(10):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1300:
                break
            a = normalize_a(make_random_smooth(n, rng))
            sc = exact_score(a)
            update_top(sc, a, top_scores, top_as)

    if top_as[0] is None or not np.isfinite(top_scores[0]):
        best_a = normalize_a(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    else:
        best_a = top_as[0].copy()
        best_sc = float(top_scores[0])

    print("Initial best: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # ---- LSE polishing stage.
    lse_end = t_start + 0.32 * total_budget
    a = best_a.copy()
    if top_as[1] is not None and top_scores[1] < best_sc + 0.02:
        a = top_as[1].copy()

    for beta in (100.0, 300.0, 1000.0, 3000.0, 10000.0):
        if time.monotonic() > lse_end - 20:
            break
        rem = lse_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 45:
            break
        sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, beta, lse_end, maxfun=220)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
            update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)
            print("LSE beta={:.0f} best={:.9f}".format(beta, best_sc))
        a = normalize_a(a2)

    # If a non-best parent remained strong, give it a short LSE chance.
    if parent is not None and top_scores[1] < top_scores[0] + 0.03:
        if time.monotonic() < lse_end - 30:
            a = normalize_a(resample_sequence(parent, n))
            for beta in (1000.0, 5000.0):
                if time.monotonic() > lse_end - 15:
                    break
                sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, beta, lse_end, maxfun=140)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)
                    print("parent LSE beta={:.0f} best={:.9f}".format(beta, best_sc))
                a = normalize_a(a2)

    # ---- Main exact multiplicative polishing.
    mde_end = t_start + 0.72 * total_budget
    sc, a2 = multiplicative_polish(best_a, mde_end, rng)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc)
        best_a = a2.copy()
    update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)
    print("After main MDE: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # ---- Secondary exact polish on a strong alternate if it exists.
    if len(top_as) >= 2 and top_as[1] is not None and top_as[1] is not best_a:
        alt_end = t_start + 0.82 * total_budget
        if time.monotonic() < alt_end - 30 and top_scores[1] < best_sc + 0.03:
            sc, a2 = multiplicative_polish(top_as[1], alt_end, rng)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a2.copy()
            update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)
            print("After alt MDE: {:.9f}".format(best_sc))

    # ---- Bounded restarts.
    restart_end = t_start + 0.91 * total_budget
    n_restarts = 0
    while time.monotonic() < restart_end - 40 and n_restarts < 6:
        n_restarts += 1
        left = float(restart_end - time.monotonic())
        chunk = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, left / float(max(1, 6 - n_restarts + 1))) - 5.0
        a = perturb_best(best_a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35)))
        sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, 5000.0, chunk, maxfun=90)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
        sc, a2 = multiplicative_polish(a2, min(chunk, restart_end - 10), rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
        update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)
        print("Restart {} best={:.9f}".format(n_restarts, best_sc))

    # ---- Final cleanup / exact polish.
    final_deadline = deadline - 8.0
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 5:
        sc, a2 = multiplicative_polish(best_a, final_deadline, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
        update_top(best_sc, best_a, top_scores, top_as)

    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(10240, dtype=np.float64) / 10240.0
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_sc

    print(
        "FINAL internal={:.9f} verified={:.9f} length={} time={:.2f}s restarts={}"
        .format(best_sc, verified, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start, n_restarts)
    )
    return out
```
